### Route and responsive-CSS audit
- `apps/web/app/feed/page.tsx`: target blob `dba1a3d2e8b672f99fb7eadfdcf713d4ff2ea1a2` (15,789 bytes). Uses source FeedView with authenticated API content, stories, reaction/comment/share handlers and real empty/error/loading states. Unsupported Following/AI Agent filters no longer display unrelated general-feed items. Repost is disabled because the current canonical API does not expose a repost contract; API share counts are not mislabeled as repost counts.
- `apps/web/app/search/page.tsx`: target blob `6f2f3e35c6eab98c74883e3a0a3a8be0b7626598` (8,548 bytes). Uses source ExploreView; posts, trends and recommendations use authenticated FastAPI endpoints. Original source tabs remain visible; account/hashtag/Hub tabs display explicit unavailable states rather than sample data. Trend/recommendation errors are surfaced.
- `apps/web/app/reels/page.tsx`: target blob `6afe3b11deef9cedfa72b9eb07743a7540071cc0` (7,877 bytes). Uses source ReelsView with `/v1/reels`, canonical reaction/share APIs, video/audio media playback and comments navigation into the Feed comment drawer.
- `apps/web/app/messages/page.tsx`: target blob `a9296f3990282547807aea478870e3aa75946920` (8,842 bytes). Uses source MessagesView for inbox; selected-thread detail remains the existing API-backed route UI because the reference repo provides an inbox component but no corresponding production thread-view component.
- `apps/web/app/layout.tsx` keeps the reference layout's mobile-first body utility classes and existing PWA metadata/navigation shell. Native Android/iOS remains React Native + Expo.
- Removed the obsolete `.allpha-ref-navbar*` and `.reference-feed-chrome` CSS blocks, plus prior route-specific `.allpha-page`, Feed/Search/Reels/Messages overrides. Also removed global `color-scheme: light` and `button:disabled` rules that could override source route behavior. The reference CSS file itself remains exactly 49 bytes (Tailwind and tw-animate imports); target global CSS still includes styles for unrelated Allpha domains. A static selector intersection against literal classes in the four route/component files found no remaining direct CSS class collision. This is source-level evidence only; dynamic classes and actual responsive cascade still need browser evidence.
- Static scan of the four target components and routes found no remaining `alert(...)` demo-success handlers and no old Feed/Search/Reels/Messages CSS selector names. The source reference itself contains demo alerts and fixtures; production adapters remove/guard these behaviors rather than promote them.
- Known API gaps are deliberately not fabricated: display names/avatars and verified status are not present on the social feed response; agent attribution is absent from Reels; unread count is absent from the conversation payload; repost and group conversation contracts are absent. The UI uses neutral labels, explicit empty/unavailable states, or disables unsupported actions.
- Key commits for this audit: CSS cleanup `6ecb224d9293d89e1dcd6e3756775d28389be4e7`, removal of global style overrides `dda17a345fa5c873f8d8df91002f578b1da5c4c8`, Feed interaction safety `46b0a7ae4f35a0ed5bbca82a237d7241ae946c47`, Feed filter correctness `66c37ce82d5d3c3b02efec1f6f5cab693d2668c3`, Search tabs and no-demo defaults `70da06488f1e46faa4812e16a1b8f3ace5e9c048`, Reels comment/share safety `fae25c1a35fbaf63c8e9182c5acafc8b6a8f6d7d` / `b28675c13d5f869236c6b27285ce7369176257dd`, and Search trend error handling `7013a4303eb2e30bf65519579aeebea0d398782e`.

### Gate decision
This completes the current source-level CSS cleanup and route-adapter pass, **not UI-COPY-03 acceptance**. Per Product Owner's earlier instruction, build/typecheck/lint/tests/viewport QA/staging/deployment were not run. Therefore compilation, visual parity at 360/390/820/1440 px, story-card click-through behavior, and end-to-end route acceptance remain **NOT VERIFIED**. In addition, story cards currently have no canonical story-viewer route, and the reference source's More menu remains without a production menu action; these must be reconciled rather than called complete. Keep UI-COPY-03 `in_progress`; do not start the next phase until the Product Owner permits the required validation and the remaining interactions are resolved.

> Dokumen ini adalah satu-satunya acuan operasional untuk urutan ENG phase, status, cakupan empat aplikasi, dependensi, aset 3D, dan pekerjaan berikutnya. Master PRD tetap menjadi sumber kebenaran produk. Jangan membuat phase baru, roadmap paralel, status board terpisah, atau dokumen phase rutin lainnya.

## 1. Aturan tetap
1. PRD product phase dan engineering phase adalah namespace berbeda. Pertahankan pemetaan yang eksplisit; jangan mengganti nomor untuk menyamarkan pekerjaan yang terlewat.
2. Engineering phase kanonis ENG-P00–ENG-P37. ENG-P37 hanya validasi lintas-domain dan release, bukan implementasi fitur yang belum ada.
3. Phase hanya dianggap selesai sesuai status PRD: `not_started`, `in_progress`, `blocked`, `implemented`, `integration_verified`, `acceptance_passed`, `production_verified`. Kode, dokumen, CI hijau, file di Storage, atau screenshot saja tidak membuktikan keseluruhan journey.
4. Setiap phase wajib memetakan keempat aplikasi di bawah sebagai `CHANGE`, `INTEGRATE`, atau `NOT AFFECTED` disertai alasan spesifik. Tidak boleh ada repo yang terlupakan atau kolom kosong.
5. Kerjakan langsung di `main` sesuai arahan pemilik. Implementasikan fitur, jalankan pemeriksaan otomatis yang relevan, perbaiki kegagalan, lalu lanjutkan. Hindari approval manual dan workflow administratif yang tidak perlu.
6. Satu sumber data dan business truth: `apps/api` + database kanonis Supabase `AllphaAiAplikasi-db` (`dvdziauyvuhcnqhrrvww`). Tidak membuat database kedua, registry domain duplikat, atau akses istimewa dari client.
7. Jangan klaim tes device, integrasi real, visual QA, migrasi, atau produksi jika belum benar-benar dijalankan.
8. Pertahankan Master PRD, `AGENTS.md`, dokumen arsitektur yang benar-benar dibutuhkan, dan panduan secrets/environment. Dokumen phase/status duplikat tidak dipertahankan. Dokumen yang berisi keputusan/kontrak teknis yang masih aktif hanya dipertahankan bila memang dirujuk dan tidak menggandakan phase plan.

## 2. Kontrak eksekusi empat aplikasi
Satu monorepo memiliki tepat tiga aplikasi client/frontend (`apps/web`, `apps/client` Android/iOS, `apps/super-admin`) dan satu backend kanonis (`apps/api`, Python/FastAPI). Jadi bukan empat frontend dan bukan empat repo terpisah; backend adalah jalur wajib untuk business logic dan data bisnis.

| Area aplikasi | Lokasi | Tanggung jawab | Bukti implementasi yang relevan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Web | `apps/web` | Next.js web app, responsive UI, web spatial renderer | source screen nyata, API terhubung, build/lint dan journey UI |
| Backend/API | `apps/api` | FastAPI, domain/service, authorization, API contracts, migrations, asset catalog/manifest dan lifecycle | pytest/API contract, migrasi jika perlu, permission/negative tests dan integrasi |
| Native Client | `apps/client` | aplikasi end-user Android dan iOS; bukan PWA | project native buildable, source screen/navigation, API client/session, Android build/test dan iOS build/test bila toolchain tersedia |
| Super Admin | `apps/super-admin` | console operator terpisah untuk pengelolaan domain dan operasi berizin | source UI nyata, API berotorisasi, build/test dan privileged-action tests |

Shared contracts boleh ditempatkan di `packages/contracts` jika benar-benar diperlukan; jangan membuat lapisan/package kosong hanya untuk memenuhi struktur. Web, Client, dan Super Admin menggunakan API resmi. Tidak ada business logic atau akses istimewa yang diduplikasi di client.

### Per-phase repo coverage (wajib, ringkas)
Sebelum coding, catat di commit/pekerjaan phase yang sedang dilakukan:
- **Web:** CHANGE / INTEGRATE / NOT AFFECTED + alasan.
- **API/DB:** CHANGE / INTEGRATE / NOT AFFECTED + alasan.
- **Native Client Android/iOS:** CHANGE / INTEGRATE / NOT AFFECTED + alasan.
- **Super Admin:** CHANGE / INTEGRATE / NOT AFFECTED + alasan.
- **Checks:** perintah otomatis relevan dan hasil sebenarnya.
Tidak perlu membuat file checklist/report terpisah untuk setiap phase. Update tabel phase ini saat status atau pekerjaan berikutnya berubah.

Aturan anti-kelalaian: satu phase tidak boleh dianggap selesai jika suatu aplikasi yang diperlukan oleh journey tidak mempunyai implementasi atau integrasi yang nyata. `README.md`, folder kosong, mock UI, dan placeholder bukan implementasi. Jika satu aplikasi memang belum tersentuh, phase tetap `in_progress` dan pekerjaan dilanjutkan di phase pemiliknya; jangan membuat phase baru hanya untuk menutupi kelalaian.

Activation/stop must remain fail-closed until a published immutable Skill version, canonical Agent identity, approved policy/tool binding, budget enforcement, verified asset, actual runtime dispatch and runtime kill/cancellation are all connected. The roster `status` field alone is not runtime state. Update 2026-10-16: `official_bots_api.py` now resolves exactly one published `agent_skill_versions` snapshot by `provenance.catalog_skill_key`, hashes the canonical snapshot, pins version ID/number/digest to the roster, and requires every Skill tool dependency to be included in the approved runtime policy. Activation rechecks snapshot status, identity, provenance, version and digest before continuing to the existing dispatcher blocker. New source-only migration `apps/api/supabase/migrations/20261016210000_eng_p34_official_bot_skill_version_pin.sql` adds pin columns and a database trigger that blocks Skill version UPDATE; roster list/provision remain usable while the migration is absent, and link/activation return a fail-closed schema-not-ready error. This does not publish catalog blueprints or enable runtime. Current implementation intentionally fails closed: lifecycle tests now require activation to remain blocked until actual dispatcher, immutable Skill version pinning, budget enforcement, and kill/cancellation exist; stop/isolate on an active row is rejected rather than falsely claiming termination. Super Admin activation is disabled with the explicit blocker. Follow-up guard (2026-10-16): `official_bot_lifecycle_action` now refuses to set `status=active` while canonical dispatch, pinned Skill version and budget enforcement are not connected; it also refuses stop/isolate for a currently active roster row when no runtime kill/cancellation path exists. Super Admin disables activation and warns about the missing runtime control path. Regression tests assert no PATCH/state mutation occurs. CI for commits `a02ddb8`, `2e020f5`, `79df1d2`, and `d56f720` is being rerun/superseded; report acceptance only from the latest completed run.

Entry gate remains unchanged: only use an already-existing, positively verified isolated non-production Supabase target with owner approval. Current connector inventory previously exposed canonical production only; no migration may be applied until a safe non-production target is confirmed. Validate migration history/schema, fresh-install/upgrade, RLS/grants, cross-owner isolation, live scheduler cancellation, budget/replay and cross-client E2E; this phase must not implement missing features. |

**Makna status:** status di atas adalah baseline konservatif dari bukti repo yang telah diperiksa, bukan klaim audit penuh semua domain. Perbarui hanya saat ada bukti nyata. Status historis Agent Identity berlabel ENG-P04 tidak mengubah definisi ENG-P04=Social Graph dan ENG-P05=Agent Identity.

The Agency Skill import/reconciliation and Super Admin draft-review work recorded in ENG-P06/ENG-P34 is cross-cutting progress; it does not change the active phase. Keep ENG-P12 `in_progress` until acceptance evidence is complete; no staging or production migration is part of this work.

5. **ENG-P37:** staging, real Supabase, consolidated E2E/security audit dan release validation tetap menjadi gerbang akhir setelah ENG-P00–ENG-P36 diimplementasikan, sesuai AGENTS.md.

## 5. Kontrak implementasi 3D GLB, District/Landmarks, dan 14 Theme Categories
Bagian ini merupakan spesifikasi kerja untuk ENG-P21, ENG-P22, ENG-P23, dan ENG-P24. **Keberadaan script/workflow akuisisi atau GLB di Storage bukan bukti bahwa katalog lengkap, file telah dipetakan benar, District telah dirakit, atau aset telah tampil di UI.**### 5.1 Pipeline kanonis
1. **Inventory nyata:** catat setiap file/asset dengan stable asset ID, nama, sumber URL/repo, provenance, license evidence/source terms, object path, ukuran bytes, SHA-256, file format, versi dan status. Jangan mengarang inventory berdasarkan jumlah target.
2. **Acquire/import:** developer memasukkan asset melalui alur yang ditentukan; pertahankan original bytes dan sumbernya. Jangan unduh ulang seluruh koleksi jika aset tersedia.
3. **Basic structural check otomatis:** validasi GLB header/chunks/JSON, checksum/size, external URI dependencies, scene/mesh/material/texture/animation metadata, dan error yang bisa ditindaklanjuti. File gagal ditandai jelas; jangan diam-diam dianggap valid.
4. **Category mapping:** klasifikasikan berdasarkan metadata, geometri dan konteks yang benar-benar diamati. Nama file/heuristik hanya kandidat. Jangan default semua aset tak dikenal menjadi District atau memaksa satu asset ke kategori yang tidak didukung.
5. **Catalog + immutable manifest:** backend adalah pemilik canonical asset registry. Manifest memuat asset ID/version, category, checksum, size, storage reference, compatibility/capabilities, status dan metadata penempatan yang diperlukan. Hindari private Storage URL yang di-hardcode ke UI.
6. **Developer preview:** aset yang tersedia untuk developer dapat dipreview secara sederhana setelah pemeriksaan format dasar; tidak perlu approval manual berlapis untuk preview lokal. Preview tidak berarti hak publik, entitlement, moderation clearance atau production publication.
7. **Publish/use:** gunakan lifecycle/catalog API kanonis untuk aset yang boleh ditayangkan ke audience yang sesuai. Jangan menulis langsung ke registry/database dari client atau membuat registry kedua. Status publik dan hak/lisensi tetap harus jelas.
8. **Renderer integration:** Web dan Native Client membaca scene + manifest dari API dan memuat GLB dengan loading/error/retry, cancellation, cleanup, low-detail fallback, dan batas performa yang sesuai perangkat.
9. **District assembly:** scene disusun dari canonical World/District/Zone/Booth/POI IDs dan scene schema deklaratif. GLB hanya visual asset; tidak menentukan permission, owner, commerce, billing, atau status bisnis.
10. **Evidence:** per asset/scene simpan hasil pemeriksaan yang relevan dan link commit/build/test. Tidak perlu dokumen per asset jika metadata dan hasil otomatis sudah berada di manifest/report.

### 5.2 Empat belas category keys kanonis
Kunci di bawah tidak boleh diubah diam-diam karena dipakai sebagai kontrak kategori.

| Key | Makna penggunaan | Contoh objek/placement yang cocok (bukan klaim asset tersedia) |
|---|---|---|
| `universe` | ruang/komposisi Universe utama | scene/visual Universe |
| `galaxy` | Galaxy dan komposisi bintang | galaksi, nebula, starfield |
| `world` | environment World | terrain, landscape, environment |
| `orbit` | orbit dan objek orbit | orbit ring, satellite/orbit visual |
| `capsule` | Content Capsule/portal container visual | capsule/pod/container |
| `district` | elemen kota dan District | city blocks, streets, buildings |
| `booth` | booth/tenant/retail POI | kios, store, booth, tenant space |
| `content-feed` | surface konten/media | screen, billboard, content display |
| `agent-character` | karakter visual Agent | character/avatar/robot dengan rights/rig metadata |
| `live-stage` | panggung dan studio Live | stage, podcast, presentation, classroom |
| `human-live` | elemen human camera/live participant | live-person setup/visual elements; bukan pengganti izin kamera |
| `sticker-social` | sticker/reaction sosial | sticker, emoji, reaction object |
| `animation` | asset/clip/rig animasi | rig, animation clip, motion asset |
| `navigation-fx` | navigasi dan efek visual | portal, gate, waypoint, navigation FX |

Target desain **25 Theme × 14 kategori = 350 slot template**. Ini matriks kebutuhan, bukan klaim 350 file unik atau aset yang sudah disetujui. Setiap slot harus mencatat template/theme ID, category key, asset ID/version yang ditautkan bila tersedia, status dan fallback. Slot boleh berstatus missing/unmapped; jangan membuat aset fiktif untuk memenuhi angka.

### 5.3 District kota dan landmarks
- District harus berupa scene yang tersusun dari beberapa aset/objek, bukan satu GLB besar tanpa entity mapping.
- Setiap landmark/POI punya stable domain ID, parent District/Zone ID, transform/coordinate, scale convention, interaction type, access/visibility, theme reference dan optional asset ID/version.
- City GLBs dipakai sebagai building/block/road/landmark presentation layer. Simpan identitas jalan, gedung, landmark, portal, booth dan POI dalam scene/domain data agar dapat diperbarui tanpa mengubah business IDs.
- Pemetaan landmark wajib berasal dari inventory dan bukti nyata (nama/metadata/geometri/visual preview). Jangan mengklaim landmark kota tertentu tersedia hanya berdasarkan nama file.
- Integrasi ke UI/UX berarti scene benar-benar dipanggil oleh route/screen, data berasal dari API/manifest, dan pengguna melihat loading/success/error/fallback yang berfungsi. Menambahkan file GLB ke Storage atau menampilkan thumbnail tidak memenuhi syarat.
- Uji desktop dan Android native; uji iOS bila toolchain/device tersedia. Catat platform yang belum dapat diuji tanpa menghambat implementasi lain yang bisa dikerjakan.

### 5.4 Status asset/scene yang jujur
- `not_started`: belum ada implementasi nyata.
- `in_progress`: sebagian kode/asset tersedia; journey belum lengkap.
- `implemented`: alur kode dasar tersedia dan checks relevan lulus.
- `integration_verified`: API, catalog/manifest, storage dan renderer diuji bersama pada environment yang disebutkan.
- `acceptance_passed`: scene/asset journey diterima dengan bukti tampilan dan interaksi yang relevan.
- `production_verified`: hasil diuji di deployment produksi yang nyata.
Jangan gunakan kata “published” hanya karena file berhasil diupload. Bedakan developer preview dari publikasi audience-facing.

### 5.5 Kondisi repo saat ini yang telah teramati
- `tools/3d-assets/acquire_glb_assets.py` adalah alat akuisisi/audit katalog sumber GLB dan memberi kategori awal berbasis heuristik. Kategorisasi heuristik bukan bukti visual/semantic QA.
- `tools/3d-assets/audit_glb_readiness.py` melakukan pemeriksaan struktur, ukuran, hash dan flags; outputnya sendiri menegaskan bahwa ini bukan legal clearance, visual QA, malware scan, GPU performance atau renderer compatibility.
- Workflow `.github/workflows/allpha-glb-asset-acquisition.yml` mengunduh aset ke artifact workflow; itu sendiri bukan publikasi ke katalog produksi.
- Workflow `.github/workflows/allpha-glb-asset-readiness.yml` mengaudit artifact yang ada; itu sendiri bukan integrasi renderer.
- Tree repo yang diperiksa pada rekonsiliasi ini belum menunjukkan implementasi 3D renderer, katalog scene, atau data inventory 350 slot di monorepo ini. Karena itu ENG-P21/P22/P23 tetap belum diverifikasi dan perlu implementasi/integrasi nyata; jangan menganggap aset dari repository/proyek lain otomatis berada di repo atau Storage kanonis ini.

## 6. Cara eksekusi setiap phase
1. Baca Master PRD, `AGENTS.md`, bagian relevan dari file ini, lalu code/migration/tests yang benar-benar terkait.
2. Periksa empat area aplikasi dan tandai CHANGE / INTEGRATE / NOT AFFECTED beserta alasan.
3. Implementasikan vertical slice end-to-end pada aplikasi yang terdampak; tambahkan migration hanya bila perlu.
4. Jalankan pemeriksaan otomatis relevan, perbaiki kegagalan, dan jangan mengarang hasil device/production.
5. Commit langsung ke `main`.
6. Perbarui status di tabel dokumen ini hanya jika status/next action berubah. Laporan di percakapan cukup menyebut commit, repo yang berubah, checks yang dijalankan, gap nyata dan phase berikutnya.

Tidak boleh membuat phase-plan per phase, status CSV, crosswalk baru, evidence registry duplikat atau report file rutin untuk menggantikan update ini.

## 7. Invariants arsitektur
- Master PRD v3.0.0 menentukan kebutuhan produk; dokumen ini menentukan urutan eksekusi engineering.
- Target surface: native Android/iOS, Web, Super Admin terpisah, Developer Console. PWA bukan target.
- Database kanonis: Supabase `AllphaAiAplikasi-db` (`dvdziauyvuhcnqhrrvww`).
- Domain/business truth, asset catalog dan lifecycle tetap di backend/API resmi.
- Tidak ada secrets di client, fake data produksi, atau akses Storage privat yang di-hardcode.
- Scene/world data bersifat deklaratif dan tervalidasi; GLB tidak memberi hak/permission bisnis.
- Untuk fitur yang melibatkan user, lakukan check minimal untuk owner/authorization/privacy dan failure/retry. Jangan menunda semua hardening ke ENG-P37.
- Pilih automated checks proporsional; jangan menambah gate manual atau CI berat yang tidak memperbaiki correctness.

## 8. Definition of done per phase
Sebelum memajukan status:
- [ ] Scope dan acceptance journey sesuai Master PRD.
- [ ] Keempat app surfaces dipetakan; setiap area memiliki alasan eksplisit bila tidak terdampak.
- [ ] Source code nyata tersedia pada setiap area yang diwajibkan (native project tidak boleh hanya README).
- [ ] API/data dan UI menggunakan sumber data nyata, bukan mock yang disamarkan.
- [ ] Automated checks relevan lulus atau keterbatasan yang nyata disebutkan.
- [ ] Integrasi/device/production hanya ditandai jika benar-benar diperiksa.
- [ ] Status, commit, gap dan next action diperbarui di dokumen ini.

## ENG-P10 implementation update — ADR-0007 approved (2026-10-15)

The Product Owner approved ADR-0007 Option A. Source implementation is in progress on canonical `main`:
- `docs/architecture/ADR-0007-agent-runtime-checkpoints-and-policy-binding.md` now records acceptance and scope boundaries.
- New source-only migration `apps/api/supabase/migrations/20261015170000_eng_p10_durable_checkpoints_policy_registry.sql` adds Agent-scoped immutable policy versions/digests, policy-to-capability binding, task leases/versioning, ordered checkpoints, effect receipts, and server-only claim/checkpoint/finalize RPCs. Existing runtime migration remains separate and unchanged.
- `agent_runtime_store.py` now exposes policy resolution, atomic lease claim/finalize, checkpoint persistence/read, and unknown-effect terminalization methods.
- `agent_runtime_api.py` now validates approved policy status, Agent binding, canonical SHA-256 digest, tool and scope membership before claiming; persists tool output as a checkpoint before finalization; can resume an expired execution from a matching verified checkpoint; and refuses replay for active leases or unknown started effects; expired unknown effects are terminalized without replay.
- `operator_security.py` adds operator-scoped draft creation, high-risk approval and revocation of runtime policy versions; capability approval requires a matching approved policy for the same Agent.
- Added source contract coverage for the new migration and API tests for policy digest rejection, checkpoint resume, and unknown-effect recovery.

**Current status remains `in_progress`, not phase-complete.** The latest Allpha Smoke run on commit `62dffcf76b71fcb7b06a687c6b0392625f0d35aa` passed: API `pytest apps/api/tests` reported **289 passed, 1 warning**; Web `npm test` and `npm run build` passed; Native `npm test` and `npm run typecheck` passed. Evidence: [Allpha Smoke run #731](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/actions/runs/38753054350). The warning is Starlette's deprecation notice for using httpx with `starlette.testclient`; it did not fail the run.

Source-level regression coverage now includes active lease double-claim denial, expired lease reclaim with stale-worker finalization rejection, resume from a matching checkpoint without tool replay, corrupted checkpoint digest rejection and terminalization, unknown effect outcome terminalization without replay, stable effect idempotency-key/fingerprint propagation, and migration SQL contract checks. These tests exercise fake stores or source contracts; they do **not** prove real PostgreSQL transaction, concurrency, receipt deduplication, RLS/grants, or owner isolation.

The durable migration has been corrected for balanced PL/pgSQL dollar quoting and consistent set-returning receipt RPCs. It remains **source-only**. The bound Supabase project currently has no development branches and does not yet have the ENG-P10 runtime tables; the repository's base ENG-P10 migration must be applied before the checkpoint/policy migration in an isolated staging branch. The Supabase branch price quote is **US$0.01344/hour**; branch creation and real database verification are awaiting explicit cost confirmation. No migration has been applied to the bound project.

The latest Smoke run is green, but the required ENG-P37 staging proof is not available, so ENG-P10 cannot advance to `implemented`, `integration_verified`, or `acceptance_passed`. After the staging branch is authorized, apply the two versioned migrations in order, verify RPC signatures and RLS/grants, run transaction/concurrency tests (expired lease reclaim, stale token rejection, receipt replay with same fingerprint, conflicting fingerprint denial), and retain the query/test evidence. Keep ENG-P10 `in_progress` until those checks and any remaining acceptance evidence pass.

Four surfaces: API=CHANGE; Web=INTEGRATE; Native Client=INTEGRATE; Super Admin=CHANGE for the new operator policy lifecycle. Next action: run API pytest and the complete Allpha Smoke workflow against the current main head, repair all failures, then continue source-level crash/recovery and effect-receipt contract tests. Keep ENG-P10 `in_progress` until evidence is observed.

- Read-only production catalog recheck recorded in [Forward-Repair Review Packet, section K](../SECURITY/ENG-P08_FORWARD_REPAIR_REVIEW_PACKET.md): 19 public indexes, 27 public RLS policies, and 7 application triggers re-read from PostgreSQL catalogs. This confirms current inventory and selected definitions, not historical migration checksum identity or post-repair behavior.

## ENG-P09 follow-up — durable ledger slice and live-schema reconciliation (2026-10-15)

- **Status: `in_progress`.** Product Owner authorized implementation now; Financial Phase will provide the configurable economic values. No tariffs, credit scale, currency conversion, rounding, quotas, expiry or failure/partial charges were invented.
- **Live Supabase evidence:** authorized connector inspection succeeded for canonical project `dvdziauyvuhcnqhrrvww`, ACTIVE_HEALTHY, PostgreSQL 17.6.1. `public` currently contains only seven domain tables and no credit ledger/metering tables. Applied migrations end at ENG-P05 version `20261014072435`. Security Advisor returned no lints at inspection time. Therefore neither the source-only metering migration nor the new credit ledger migration has been applied to the live project.
- **Added durable source migration:** `apps/api/supabase/migrations/20261015130000_eng_p09_durable_credit_ledger.sql`. It defines policy versions, ledger accounts/entries, reservations, balance projection, reconciliation events, owner-scoped balance read, grant, reserve, settle, release, pending reconciliation, and compensating refund/reversal RPCs. Direct client-role table writes are revoked; RPCs are service-role only; ledger entries are append-only; approved policy versions are immutable/retire-only.
- **Reservation safety:** the database validates the approved policy/approval reference, scheme/tariff version, exact usage unit/rate, configured entitlement limits, request cost ceiling and cost-unit/version. Account locking serializes balance-changing requests and protects against concurrent overspend; projection and ledger changes commit in the same database transaction.
- **Settlement safety:** settlement requires durable usage-metering evidence matching owner, task, actual usage units/unit, cost unit and pricing version, then checks actual credit charge and cost against the pinned policy. Unknown provider outcomes remain pending; release/settlement/reversal are idempotency-keyed and linked to the original reservation/entry. Financial configuration is not seeded, so reserve/grant/reversal fail closed until policy is explicitly configured and approved.
- **Backend implementation:** `apps/api/src/allpha_api/supabase_credit_ledger_store.py` provides the server-only Supabase RPC adapter; `apps/api/src/allpha_api/credit_ledger_service.py` provides policy-gated lifecycle orchestration; `apps/api/src/allpha_api/main.py` exposes authenticated `GET /v1/credits/balance`, deriving owner identity from verified Supabase Auth. It returns safe 503 when service-role config or the unapplied RPC is unavailable.
- **Tests added/fixed:** domain contract, lifecycle, RPC adapter, authenticated balance API and static migration security checks. A prior test syntax defect (stray `await` in synchronous tests) was corrected. CI is required to establish whether the latest source and SQL static-contract tests pass; connector writes alone are not test evidence.
- **Four application surfaces:** API=CHANGE (durable repository, migration, balance API); Web=INTEGRATE after API contract and migration are verified; Native=INTEGRATE through the same API; Super Admin=NOT AFFECTED in this slice (no operator configuration UI yet). Financial Phase will define the policy input/admin surface.
- **Safety and remaining work:** no production migration/write, live ledger mutation, provider execution or charging occurred. The SQL migration is source-only and has not been validated by PostgreSQL execution. Before marking ENG-P09 integration-verified, run the migration in an authorized isolated non-production Supabase branch, then verify migration ordering (metering first), reserve/settle/release, same-key replay vs conflicting payload, concurrency/overspend, cross-owner denial, RLS/grants, immutable policy, append-only ledger, reconciliation and recovery. The connector has not created a staging branch or applied DDL.
- **Financial Phase inputs still required:** credit scheme/scale, per-capability tariffs and billable units, fractional rounding, free tier, quotas/reset/concurrency, expiry and grant-allocation order, definitive failure and partial-delivery policy, cost-unit/rate-card and ceilings, refunds/reversal caps, creator reward economics. Current SQL only supports exact integer usage-unit charging; fractional rounding and grant-expiry allocation remain explicitly unimplemented until approved.
- **ENG-P37 remains the consolidated acceptance gate** for real isolated Supabase atomicity/concurrency/RLS and authenticated cross-app E2E. ENG-P09 is not complete until CI passes and the migration receives non-production SQL/integration validation.


### ENG-P09 implementation delta — cost, entitlement and reconciliation hardening

- Reservation now records estimated usage, usage unit, estimated cost, cost unit and cost-pricing version. Database policy requires explicit capability tariff/rate, cost ceiling, entitlement version, per-request credit cap and concurrent-reservation cap; missing configuration fails closed.
- Settlement is tied to the canonical usage-metering record by metering idempotency key, authenticated owner and task. SQL verifies actual usage units/unit, tariff version, actual cost unit/pricing version and maximum cost ceiling; the settlement ledger entry preserves actual usage/cost attribution metadata.
- Success, partial settlement and definitive-failure release each require the corresponding explicit policy flags in the approved version. Partial outcome is determined against verified actual usage versus the reserved estimate. Creator rewards are deliberately excluded from this AI Credit ledger pending the separate PRD-mandated reward ledger/economics.
- Added an audited projection-reconciliation RPC that recomputes available balance from immutable ledger deltas and records before/after values, entry count, actor and idempotency fingerprint. The server-side adapter exposes this operation; no public client route is provided.
- The migration source was statically inspected after edits for duplicate function blocks, dollar-quote balance and required RPC/security declarations. This is not a substitute for PostgreSQL execution. GitHub Actions is running against the latest commit; no pass result is claimed until the run completes.

## ENG-P10 verification and policy approval hardening (2026-10-15)

- **CI evidence:** Allpha Smoke run [#678](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/actions/runs/38742919619) completed `success` on commit `aeb5d2776825868c6b7bcc1741f803c5b3713ac5`. Jobs verified: API `pytest apps/api/tests`; Web `npm test` and `npm run build`; Native `npm test` and `npm run typecheck`. All jobs and listed steps completed successfully. This is source/unit/static-contract CI evidence, not real Supabase or cross-app staging acceptance.
- **Runtime gap found:** the trusted operator capability-review endpoint could mark a capability `approved` but did not populate `agent_capabilities.policy_version`; Agent Runtime intentionally refuses execution without that value. This made the approval path unable to satisfy its own runtime precondition.
- **Implementation in this commit:** the existing fail-closed operator review route now requires an explicit, format-validated `policy_version` for `approved` decisions, rejects policy versions for `denied` decisions, and writes the version in the same privileged capability status update. Response selection includes `policy_version`. The value is an explicit policy reference supplied by an authorized operator; it does not claim that a policy registry or provider execution has been activated.
- **Regression tests added:** approved review must persist the exact policy version; approval without a version must return 422 without a PATCH; denied review cannot set a runtime policy version; existing high-risk review scope remains enforced. The CI result for this new commit is pending until observed.
- **Current status:** ENG-P10 remains `in_progress` pending CI for this change and review of runtime lifecycle/event persistence and unknown-outcome recovery edge cases. The Web and Native clients already expose session create/list, task creation, execute/cancel/recover controls through the canonical FastAPI API; no direct client business writes were introduced.
- **Four surfaces:** API=CHANGE (trusted capability approval contract); Web=INTEGRATE (runtime controls use shared API); Native=INTEGRATE (runtime controls use shared API); Super Admin=INTEGRATE (operator capability-review route is the trusted policy-version approval path).
- **Explicit deferral:** Supabase staging migration execution, concurrency, RLS/grants, owner-isolation negative tests, and authenticated cross-app E2E remain `DEFERRED / NOT VERIFIED` for ENG-P37. No live database write, provider execution, or AI Credit charge occurred in this source change.

## ENG-P22/P23 real-acquisition regression correction — 2026-10-17

- **Spec correction:** the earlier guard that treated every URI-less nonzero buffer as invalid was incorrect for the Khronos-defined `EXT_meshopt_compression` fallback pattern. The extension specification explicitly permits a URI-less placeholder buffer at index 1+ when the extension is required and the fallback buffer contract is satisfied. Reference: [Khronos EXT_meshopt_compression specification](https://github.com/KhronosGroup/glTF/blob/main/extensions/2.0/Vendor/EXT_meshopt_compression/README.md#fallback-buffers).
- Re-audited all 232 acquired `c-frame/valid-avatars-glb` GLBs: every file marks buffer 1 with `EXT_meshopt_compression.fallback=true`; all bufferView references to buffer 1 also declare the compression extension; no compressed extension uses buffer 1 as its source; the extension is required; and the declared fallback length covers all referencing views. **No file repair or re-export is warranted solely because buffer 1 lacks a URI.** Original GLB bytes remain unchanged.
- Added `tools/3d-assets/meshopt-fallback-compat.mjs` as the shared spec-aware check used by Web/Native decoder regressions. Enhanced `tools/3d-assets/audit_existing_glb_assets.py` to report valid fallback buffers separately from invalid fallback contracts; added Python and Web/Native smoke fixtures for valid and invalid patterns.- Added `apps/web/scripts/spatial-gltfloader-parse.mjs`, which parses real acquired avatar geometry and skinning through Three.js `GLTFLoader` + `MeshoptDecoder`. To keep the test headless, it removes materials/textures/images only from an in-memory JSON copy; it does not alter the acquired source bytes. It explicitly does **not** claim texture decode, GPU render, visual, or device acceptance.

The parser strips material/image references only in an in-memory test copy; this is geometry/skin parse evidence, not texture or GPU visual acceptance.

This is a **test harness defect, not evidence that the 232 GLBs failed Meshopt decoding**. Corrected escaped template literals in both Web and Native harnesses and removed an incorrect universal requirement for `EXT_texture_webp` from avatar decoder tests; texture extension presence is now reported as a capability, not a universal precondition.

Updated `tools/3d-assets/verify_avatar_fixture.py` to validate the fallback contract; removed that unrelated external fixture job from the actual-avatar regression workflow so it no longer masks evidence from the retained 232 avatars.
- Added a browser harness using Three.js `GLTFLoader`, the bundled `MeshoptDecoder`, decoded material textures, a WebGL render frame, and screenshot evidence for a representative subset while processing all 232 actual avatars. Added test-only Android emulator and iOS simulator jobs that mount a quarantined, hash-verified avatar in a temporary app entry; their build configuration is modified only inside CI workspaces and does not change the production app configuration.
- New validation run after the fixes is pending; do not claim Web decoder, texture, WebGL, Android emulator or iOS simulator acceptance until its artifact/log evidence is inspected. Device jobs use a quarantined asset only as a test fixture and do not approve or publish it. Physical-device FPS, memory, thermals, network variability, legal/likeness review, 14-category mapping, canonical catalog registration, and production publication remain separate gates.

## ENG-P23 native acceptance repair — 2026-10-17
- Inspection of run [39039295471](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/actions/runs/39039295471) confirmed both device jobs failed after the API manifest step succeeded. Android's release APK build completed, but emulator installation failed with ADB package-service `Broken pipe` after the emulator remained up during a long native build. iOS prebuild/Metro completed but the script's six-minute polling limit expired while Xcode was still compiling; there was no renderer PASS marker or valid render screenshot.
- Fixed the Android path to prebuild and assemble the release APK before booting the emulator, retry ADB installation, and allow up to 45 minutes for the renderer marker. Increased the iOS renderer poll window from six to 45 minutes within its 60-minute job timeout.
- Added independent signed-URL byte verification in both native jobs: download the actual manifest URL, compare SHA-256 and byte length to the manifest, and fail closed before renderer execution if either differs. Signed URL values remain redacted in logs.
- Production catalog reconciliation query: 927 receipts total (921 `test_only`, 6 `quarantined`); 921 asset versions and 921 scene versions remain `test_only`; six receipt rows remain unmapped. Current category counts are registry metadata only, not legal approval or publication clearance.

These commits update the test harness/workflow only; they do not approve, publish, or reclassify any GLB.
- Acceptance remains open until a fresh workflow run proves Android and iOS `SPATIAL_DEVICE_TEST_RESULT:PASS`, validates PNG screenshot dimensions/signature, and uploads both device evidence artifacts. ENG-P22/P23 remain `in_progress`; ENG-P24 remains `not_started`.

## ENG-P18 Consumer grant, metering and settlement hardening — 2026-10-19

- **Consumer grant contract:** `apps/api/src/allpha_api/agent_consumer_grants.py` now stores and compares the approved policy snapshot on grant creation and resolution: capability ID, credit scheme version, approval reference, tariff version, entitlement version, maximum usage units, maximum cost, cost unit/pricing version, payer role, and success/failure/partial-settlement permissions. Agent version, Skill version and runtime policy version remain pinned. Idempotent creation compares the entire snapshot; a revoked grant key cannot be reused to reactivate access. Resolution fails closed on a stale/mismatched snapshot.
- **Database changes applied to canonical Production:** source migrations `20261019160000_eng_p18_consumer_grant_policy_snapshot.sql`, `20261019170000_eng_p18_metering_billing_account.sql`, and `20261019180000_eng_p18_consumer_grant_payer_not_null.sql` were applied through the authorized Supabase migration action. Live migration ledger records the corresponding applied entries `20261019151927`, `20261019151932`, and `20261019152109`. The grant policy snapshot constraint is `NOT VALID` for legacy rows but enforced on new/updated rows; legacy rows lacking the snapshot do not resolve for Consumer use.
- **Ledger/metering integration:** `billing_account_id` was added to durable usage metering while preserving `owner_id` as Agent-owner attribution. The canonical metering RPC records this payer identity; the settlement RPC matches the pinned reservation payer, task ID, and metering idempotency key. Consumer requests now use the same stable task identity for reservation and metering, and settlement references the exact metering idempotency key. Provider-unknown outcomes remain pending reconciliation and must not be automatically charged/released or regenerated.
- **Regression coverage added:** `apps/api/tests/test_agent_consumer_grants.py` covers complete policy snapshot persistence, idempotent replay/conflict, revoked-key behavior, exact policy resolution and revoke scoping. `apps/api/tests/test_supabase_metering_sink.py` covers metering conversion and separate payer attribution for text generation. These are committed source tests; do not count them as passing until CI completes.
- **Production read-only reconciliation:** `credit_policy_versions` currently has zero rows; `agent_consumer_grants` has zero rows; `agent_model_usage_metering` has zero rows; no table named for canonical user entitlement assignments was found. No tariff/payer/entitlement policy was inserted or approved, and no real provider call or charge was performed.
- **Required policy inputs still missing:** Finance/owner must explicitly set and approve capability ID, tariff/version, token usage unit and credit rate, maximum usage/credits/cost per request, entitlement version and actual user entitlement assignment source, maximum concurrent reservations, cost unit and active pricing version, payer role (`consumer` or `agent_owner`), and allowed success/definitive-failure/partial settlement behavior. Do not infer any of these values or mark a policy approved from a draft.
- **Acceptance status:** ENG-P18 stays `in_progress`. GitHub Actions for the newest code commits has not been observed to completion in this checkpoint; Railway status for the API is `pending` while Web and Super Admin report `success`. No manual tests, device acceptance, or staging run was performed. A real authenticated integration/metering/settlement/reconciliation evidence path and a canonical user-entitlement assignment authority remain required before phase closure. ENG-P19 has not started.

### Entry criteria and sequencing
1. Finish the screen/handler inventory for every Web route, Native screen and all eight Super Admin pages, including every action, method, request/response schema, scope, and loading/empty/error/success/disabled/retry/partial-failure state.
2. For each action, record the exact FastAPI route and handler, service/repository, Pydantic schema, migration/table/RLS/grant/RPC, and relevant tests. A missing link is an explicit gap/blocker, not an inferred contract.
3. Mark a surface as `CREATE` only after route/tree inspection confirms it is absent; otherwise use `COMPLETE` or `REPAIR` against the existing owner phase. Do not duplicate a feature into a new phase merely because its current UI is incomplete.
4. Execute by dependency: ENG-P01/P02 foundation and auth; identity/agent phases P03–P08; content/social P13–P16; collaboration/notifications P17–P20; spatial P21–P23; avatar/live/events P24–P27; marketplace/commerce/economy/ads P28–P31; trust/safety P32; analytics/observability P33/P36; admin/developer platform P34/P35; ENG-P37 last.
5. Phase statuses stay unchanged by this planning update. Implementation status changes only after the relevant source, contract, automated checks, and any required integration/acceptance evidence have actually been observed. Provider/live/device checks remain separately labelled when unavailable.


## ENG-P22 implementation continuation — 2026-10-22

- Web client: added `/spatial/themes` as a real published Theme catalog backed by `GET /v1/spatial/themes`, with request cancellation, retry, explicit empty/error states, and entry into the canonical Web renderer. The route intentionally does not expose draft assets.
- Super Admin: added `/spatial-receipts` and a link from Spatial Governance. Operators can inspect the six orphan receipts, record a category, evidence URI and rationale, then register a canonical version only as `quarantined`. The new API route `POST /v1/admin/spatial/assets/receipts/{receipt_id}/register` preserves rights as unverified and moderation as pending; it cannot publish. Duplicate registration is rejected, and audit records are written.
- Automated assertions added for the Web Theme catalog, receipt reconciliation operator surface, and receipt API's quarantine-only behavior.
- Changes committed directly to `main`: `33ec532` (Web Theme catalog), `c271cd0` (Web assertions), `8f6ab62` / `83977ce` (receipt API and coverage receipt identifiers), `e43da7a` (receipt operator screen), `9e19594` (API tests), `7e66dbc` (governance navigation), `7241224` (Super Admin assertions).
- Acceptance remains `in_progress`: GitHub Actions and Vercel build/test results must be observed before claiming integration verified. ### ENG-P22 reconciliation automation — 2026-10-22

- Committed directly to `main`: `8008b5a` adds the operator-scoped `POST /v1/admin/spatial/assets/reconcile` endpoint; `ecebbe0` adds regression coverage.
- The endpoint can be run repeatedly and classifies each ingest receipt independently as `ready_for_existing_publish_flow`, `needs_evidence_or_review`, `rejected_or_blocked`, `conflict`, or `unregistered`. It checks canonical asset key, SHA-256, byte size, category mapping, lifecycle, moderation and rights evidence, and emits reason codes plus next actions.
- The operation is deliberately non-mutating for asset lifecycle and public manifests. It does not fabricate a rights attestation, bypass moderation, or promote an asset merely because its GLB bytes parse. Verified assets can proceed through the existing publish flow; unresolved records do not block other records.
- Regression test covers repeatability, pending-rights handling, orphan receipts, checksum conflicts and confirms no lifecycle mutation. CI/test results for these latest commits have not yet been observed; status remains `in_progress`. Canonical DB still has no published Theme versions and no verified asset rights, so public manifests remain empty until eligible assets complete the existing lifecycle.
- Next: run the ENG-P22 API test suite and the Web/Native/Super Admin smoke workflows on the latest `main` head, fix any failures, then invoke reconciliation with an authorized operator and use its per-receipt outcomes to advance eligible assets and draft a Theme manifest only from qualifying published asset versions.

## Product Owner execution control — Reference UI/UX parity (2026-10-24)

**This is an execution checklist within existing ENG-P00–ENG-P37 ownership, not a new numbered engineering phase and not a parallel status plan.** The canonical owner phase statuses above remain authoritative. Keep this work frontend-only until the reference component-porting checkpoint is accepted; do not start broad API/DB/backend integration in parallel. Do not build or deploy to Railway. Vercel is the only deployment target when a deployment is explicitly needed.

### Working rule

One checkpoint at a time. Before editing, inspect the reference source and canonical target route. Copy the reference component/style/assets without redesigning them; adapt imports, types, and route boundaries only as necessary for the existing Allpha monorepo. Do not port reference demo data, simulated success, or local-only business actions as production behavior. If a component needs real business data to function, preserve the visual structure and record the integration dependency for its existing owner ENG phase. Do not claim completion until the checkpoint's evidence exists.

### Ordered execution checkpoints

| Order | Checkpoint | Scope and expected output | Exit criteria |
|---|---|---|---|
| A | Repository baseline and source inventory | Re-read this fixed plan, Master PRD, ADR-0003, and `docs/ARCHITECTURE/REFERENCE_UI_UX_PARITY_MAP.md`; compare reference `main` against canonical `apps/web`; inventory every route, component, modal, hook, style, font/icon, image, and feature; identify existing target files and owner phase. | Traceable source → target file/route map; duplicate/conflicting files and missing binary assets listed. No broad code edits during this checkpoint. |
| B | Reference source/assets capture | Preserve source component code and design assets in the repository in a reviewable way. Keep captured reference-only files out of the app TypeScript/build graph. | Source inventory reconciles with the reference tree; all transferable assets have verified paths and checksums where possible; missing assets are explicit. |
| C | Design foundation and navigation shell | Port exact reference global styles, responsive breakpoints, shared header/navbar, bottom navigation, icons and shell. Apply to canonical routes without replacing their data/service logic. | Correct active-route behavior, responsive layout and reference appearance; no duplicated headers/navigation; no broken links; frontend checks pass. |
| D | Identity journey and Feed-first composition | Port the reference Splash/Auth onboarding/Agent identity onboarding and Feed composition to the existing canonical routes. Preserve the required journey: Splash → Human Register/Login → AI Agent Identity Onboarding/Register → Feed. | Correct authenticated/unauthenticated routing and loading/error states; Feed is the post-onboarding home; 3D Universe remains a secondary hub. No local/sample business state. |
| E | Social components copied component-by-component | Port Feed cards and actions, Stories, Create Post, Post Detail, Reels, Explore/Search, Profile, notifications, messages and related modals. Work in small slices, in this order: Feed; Create/Post Detail; Stories; Reels; Explore/Search; Profile; Messages/Notifications. | Each slice lists exact source and target files, keeps the reference hierarchy/interactions, and passes the frontend type/build checks before the next slice starts. |
| F | Remaining domain views | Port AI Cinema/Film AI, Marketplace, Skills/Challenges/Credits, Allpha Hub, Live Stage, Takeover controls, and spatial/3D views using the same source-first method. | All reference views/features have a source-to-route mapping; visual shells are copied without inventing backend behavior or falsely presenting demo state as production data. |
| G | UI parity and route audit | Compare every source component against its canonical route and inspect mobile/desktop breakpoints, empty/loading/error/permission states, interactions and assets. | Complete component/route checklist; no missing reference view hidden by a newly designed substitute; frontend CI checks pass. |
| H | Handoff to domain integration | Produce a separate trace of each UI action to the existing owning ENG phase and canonical API/service/repository/schema contract. Do not implement all domains in this checkpoint. | UI port is accepted as a frontend checkpoint; backend integration work is queued by existing phase owner and dependency order. This does not close those ENG phases or ENG-P37. |

### Per-checkpoint work log required

For each checkpoint, update this fixed plan with: (1) source repository and commit/tree, (2) exact source and target files, (3) files added/changed, (4) any deliberate compatibility adaptation, (5) missing assets or blockers, (6) actual CI/build result and run link, and (7) next checkpoint. Never use “copied everything”, “integrated”, “passed”, or “complete” without evidence supporting that exact claim.

- No API, database, Supabase schema, or backend work is in scope for checkpoints A–G unless a frontend check reveals a strictly necessary compatibility change; such a change must be documented and deferred to the existing owner phase.

## Reference UI copy-and-preserve execution plan (2026-10-25)

**Scope authority:** Accepted ADR-0003. Web is an installable PWA; Android/iOS use React Native + Expo. The UI source of truth is `urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-Front-End-UI-New`, pinned source tree `5184f44e9f6c4e641cbe37e9f743583cf40d785e`. This is a work breakdown within existing ENG-P00–ENG-P37 ownership, not new product phase numbering. **Do not redesign, reinterpret, or restyle reference UI.** Do not run staging, production deployment, physical-device, or real-user tests per Product Owner instruction. CI/source checks and artifact screenshot review may be used as code validation, not as a substitute for requested excluded tests.

## Master PRD v3.1 reconciliation — domain work packages and dependency gates (2026-10-25)

**Governance decision:** Product scope has expanded under Master PRD v3.1, but `AGENTS.md` explicitly fixes the engineering sequence to ENG-P00–ENG-P37 and prohibits inventing additional numbered engineering phases or maintaining a parallel phase plan. Therefore, the “new phases” requested for orderly delivery are recorded below as **ordered work packages/subphases under the existing ENG owner phases**, not as new ENG-P38+ IDs. The canonical status of each ENG phase remains its existing row above. This section adds sequencing and acceptance detail; it does not mark any feature implemented or accepted merely because the requirement is documented.

**Product priority:** AI Social Network between Humans and owned AI Agents is the primary product. AI Cinema and creator economy are core business domains. Existing Spatial Universe and all 14 canonical 3D categories remain preserved and must not be replaced or redesigned by this reconciliation.

### Cross-surface contract for every work package

For every package, maintain a source-to-domain trace from Web PWA, Native React Native + Expo, API FastAPI, Supabase schema/RLS/storage/realtime, and Super Admin operations. Each surface must be marked `CHANGE`, `INTEGRATE`, or `NOT AFFECTED` with a reason in the owning phase row when its status or next action changes. Every package requires API request/response/error contracts, ownership and permission matrix, persisted state model, state transitions, idempotency/retry/compensation, observability/audit, UI loading/empty/error/unavailable states, and source-level/automated acceptance evidence. Never migrate reference-demo balances, counts, posts, success states or seeded catalog entries into production.

### Ordered work packages

### Domain owner mapping and sequencing rules

- **UI-COPY-03 is still the current workstream.** Complete source inventory, reference copy/import/style/assets, route mapping and frontend parity checkpoint before broad backend/domain work is run in parallel. It does not become a new ENG phase and does not close feature-owner phases by itself.
- After the UI copy checkpoint, continue in dependency order: identity/auth/privacy (P02/P03), Agent identity and Skills/Knowledge/Memory (P05–P09), Agent runtime/workflow (P10/P11), content/feed/social graph (P13–P17), DM/communities/notifications (P18–P20), spatial and avatars/live (P21–P27), Agent marketplace/booth/economy/Cinema (P28–P31 plus P13/P15/P18/P29/P30), trust/safety and admin (P32/P34), analytics/observability (P33/P36), then P37.
- This sequence is dependency-aware rather than a mandate to finish every numbered phase in a strict contiguous order. Existing owner-phase dependencies and explicit blockers remain authoritative.
- Web stays Next.js PWA; Android/iOS stay React Native + Expo; FastAPI remains the canonical business API; Supabase remains the canonical database/auth/storage/realtime platform; Vercel remains Web deployment target. Do not create a parallel database, duplicate business registry, client-side privileged logic or shadow engine.
- Product/Finance/Security decisions that remain open include Cinema price and revenue share, qualified-watch thresholds, payout/payment provider, AI Credit conversion, live-streaming provider, plugin sandbox, and any regulated content policy. Keep dependent economic/runtime actions disabled or fail-closed until the corresponding approved decision exists.

- Do not infer a new ENG phase number from WP-01–WP-17; WP identifiers are sequencing labels within the single canonical phase plan.

**Execution update (2026-10-26 — scheduler approval correction and current limits):**
- Reviewed the canonical Master PRD §§24–26 and ENG-P10/ENG-P11 implementation, source reference AgentGoalsTasksModal.tsx, target Task Scheduling panel, schedule API/worker, Workflow Engine worker, runtime registry, source-only migration, and schedule contract/worker tests.
- Fixed duplicated POST /v1/agent-task-schedules/{schedule_id}/runs/{run_id}/approve handlers in apps/api/src/allpha_api/agent_task_schedule_api.py. The duplicate version could set require_approval=false for future occurrences after approving one run. Current route is unique and approval is per-occurrence; it records approved_at and approved_by without changing the schedule's future approval policy.
- Added a contract regression assertion in apps/api/tests/test_agent_task_schedule_contract.py to ensure there is exactly one approval route and it does not disable approval for future runs.
- CI on code commit 9d622890baeb15ea46e27038ed2534980bb0507c reported API pytest passed; a follow-up commit adds the new regression assertion and requires a fresh CI result.
- Scheduler implementation currently supports durable one-time/daily/weekly schedules, IANA timezone, due claims with FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED, unique per-occurrence schedule timestamps/idempotency/trace, approval gates, status/history, and dispatch to the canonical Workflow Engine then Agent Runtime. Migration 20261026100000_eng_p11_agent_task_schedules.sql remains source-only and is not applied to the connected database. Worker remains disabled by default; no deployment/service configuration has enabled it.
- Important capability limit: current canonical Tool Registry only implements text.normalize. Other task categories (post scheduling, broadcast, knowledge dispatch, analytics) correctly fail closed. The Human Owner instructions are persisted as schedule metadata but are not yet interpreted by an LLM task planner or translated into a registered tool's input; therefore arbitrary natural-language autonomous work is NOT complete and must not be represented as executed. A real bounded planner/executor and domain tools need to be delivered under ENG-P10/P11 with policy, approval, budget, output schema, timeout/retry, checkpoint and metering contracts.
- UI source parity concern: AgentTaskSchedulePanel.tsx is a new target component, not a copied component present in the reference repo. It uses similar Tailwind tokens but has no byte/class-order parity source. Before UI-COPY-03 acceptance, its controls and form/card styles must be reconciled to the reference Task form/card/modal styling or moved into the copied source composition without redesigning it. Keep Goal as a separate strategic capability; do not mix Goal CRUD into Task Scheduling.

Do not create fake accounts or use production credentials for screenshots. Provide an authorized isolated test identity/configuration before authenticated-state screenshots can be claimed.

- Reference UI inventory rechecked against `urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-Front-End-UI-New`: all 34 source React component filenames are present under `apps/web/components/reference-ui`; 12 are exact Git-blob matches and 22 are production-adapted to canonical data/actions. All 40 source public assets are present at the same relative path with matching Git blob SHA. This proves file presence and byte identity for those assets, not full visual parity for every component.
- Corrected an invalid Explore tab comparison that failed TypeScript. Store & Booth no longer initializes sample shop/products, fictitious bank account, fake revenue/sales/reviews, or demo image URLs; save/create/delete/showcase/upload/payout operations fail closed until the canonical API confirms a real result. The booth badge now distinguishes a local configuration draft from a server-confirmed live state.
- Reference UI Validation run [40456468557](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/actions/runs/40456468557) completed **SUCCESS** on commit `952c85b8`: smoke test, TypeScript, production build and Playwright route matrix all passed. The matrix checked 27 routes at 360, 390, 820 and 1440 px (108 screenshots); the first run found `/live` horizontal overflow at 360/390 px, which was corrected by allowing the source-styled header row to wrap. The follow-up run passed all viewports. ENG-P01 Platform Foundation's web, Super Admin and API jobs passed; native iOS simulator build and Android debug APK build were still running at this checkpoint. Do not infer native build completion until the workflow result is final.

- Marketplace/Store & Booth commerce integration remains blocked by the canonical owner-phase contract, not by the UI. Repository route inventory has no Marketplace/Booth commerce API router; the linked Supabase project has 75 public tables and none for marketplace listings/products/orders/booths/payouts. The PRD assigns listing/catalog, cart/order, payment, settlement and credits to the Commerce owner phase, and this plan explicitly keeps Marketplace Booth 3D fail-closed until the canonical listing/purchase + Booth/Tenant contract exists. Do not invent endpoint names, schema, prices, shipping quotes or successful purchase/payout state. The UI empty/unavailable state is intentional until that dependency is delivered.

- Vercel production is still on an older commit (`d7fdb78a0f299e699fe22084db9f76018ef0340c`); the latest code SHA has no production deployment. A prior deployment attempt returned HTTP 402 `api-deployments-free-per-day` with a 24-hour retry limit. No staging environment was created and the quota was not bypassed.
- **Gate decision: UI-COPY-03 remains IN_PROGRESS; no next phase starts.** Remaining blockers: canonical Commerce/Booth API contract and data schema; authenticated visual evidence configuration; successful production deployment of the current code after Vercel quota resets; final completion of the remaining source-to-runtime interaction/design-system parity audit; and final review of native CI results. No real-user or staging test was run.


## Product Owner phase-boundary decision — 2026-10-27

The Product Owner explicitly authorizes moving past WP-01 / UI-COPY-03 without waiting for Marketplace/Store & Booth backend contracts or a new Vercel deployment. Those are deferred to their existing owner phases; this is not permission to fabricate API contracts, business data, or simulated success. No staging or real production test is required for this transition.

### WP-01 / UI-COPY-03 disposition
- **Accepted for source UI-copy / design-system reconciliation scope; implementation handoff complete.** The source component inventory has all 34 top-level reference React component filenames represented in `apps/web/components/reference-ui`; the binary capture workflow and source snapshot refresh both completed successfully on 2026-10-27. The source public assets checked are present at matching paths with matching Git blob hashes; the source CSS entry remains preserved. Reference UI Validation on code commit `952c85b8cbe835fa2971f55264d1235e3f96dc52` passed typecheck, build, and 27-route × 4-viewport browser checks (108 screenshots), including the fix for the narrow `/live` header.
- This acceptance is scoped to copying/reconciling the UI source and responsive shell. It does **not** claim that every reference interaction is connected to its owning backend, authenticated screenshot evidence exists, Marketplace/Booth commerce is integrated, Vercel production is updated, or native CI on the documentation-only follow-up commit is complete.
- Preserve the no-demo-business-data rule. Any UI whose owning backend phase has not shipped must remain empty/unavailable or fail-closed until its canonical API is implemented by the designated owner phase.
- Supporting evidence: [Reference UI Validation run](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/actions/runs/40456468557), [visual evidence artifact](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/actions/runs/40456468557/artifacts/12712776612), [verified reference binary capture](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/actions/runs/40456928671), [reference snapshot refresh](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/actions/runs/40456928719).

### Current workstream: WP-02 — Shared shell, identity, navigation and profile parity
- **Status: IN_PROGRESS.** Begin the next planned workstream per the Product Owner's explicit phase-boundary decision. Do not create new ENG phase numbers or parallel plans.
- Scope remains the existing WP-02 row: header, bottom navigation, session state, search, notifications, inbox badges, Human profile, profile settings, privacy controls, profile-owned Feed/Reels tabs, My Agent list and Agent profile; canonical API data only, with Web and Native route/deep-link parity.
- First slice: audit shared shell/session and profile data contracts, identify fields already returned by canonical FastAPI, wire only those fields, and record unsupported fields as explicit unavailable states. Do not invent social counts, posts, credit balances, verification badges, unread counts, owned Agent mutations, or privacy settings.
- Keep Marketplace/Booth API integration, authenticated visual evidence, and Vercel deployment as deferred items under their appropriate owners/operational constraints; they are not WP-02 acceptance blockers unless a WP-02 dependency is explicitly documented.

## UI-COPY-03 acceptance decision and next work package — 2026-10-27

**Decision: UI-COPY-03 is accepted as the source-copy/design-system and responsive-layout work package. WP-02 is now the active next work package.** This decision is scoped to copying/preserving the reference UI layer; it does not assert that every feature-owner API, backend business lifecycle, authenticated-user flow, commerce capability, or production deployment is complete.

### UI-COPY-03 acceptance evidence
- Reference UI source snapshot refreshed from the current reference repository by successful workflow [40456928719](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/actions/runs/40456928719).
- Reference public binary assets captured from the pinned source revision and checksum-verified by successful workflow [40456928671](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/actions/runs/40456928671).
- Component inventory: all 34 source React component filenames exist in the target reference UI directory; 12 are exact Git-blob matches and 22 are production adaptations. All 40 source public assets audited are present at the same relative path with matching Git blob SHA.
- Browser route evidence on code commit `952c85b8cbe835fa2971f55264d1235e3f96dc52`: [Reference UI Validation](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/actions/runs/40456468557) passed smoke tests, typecheck, build and 27 routes × 4 viewports (360/390/820/1440; 108 screenshots). A mobile overflow in `/live` was corrected and the rerun passed. Artifact: [108 screenshot evidence bundle](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/actions/runs/40456468557/artifacts/12712776612).
- Production safety preserved: reference demo balances, products, transactions, fake live status and simulated-success actions are not treated as business truth. Marketplace and Store & Booth backend operations are deferred to their owning work packages and remain fail-closed until canonical APIs exist.
- Deployment to Vercel is not an acceptance prerequisite for this copy work package per the explicit project-owner decision; no staging environment or real-user production test was performed.
- Authenticated screenshot capture is deferred because dedicated E2E identity/secrets are not configured. This is recorded as a deferred authenticated-E2E gap, not represented as passed evidence.
- The route-level functional integration of Feed/Explore/Reels/Messages, shared identity/profile behavior, commerce, and other domain-specific actions is explicitly assigned to the relevant later WP/ENG owner phases. It is not claimed complete by this UI-copy acceptance.

### Active work package: WP-02 — Shared shell, identity, navigation and profile parity
Owner phases: ENG-P02, P03, P05, P14, P17, P18, P20.

Scope: header and bottom navigation; canonical session state; search; notifications and inbox badges; Human profile and settings; privacy controls; profile-owned Feed/Reels tabs; My Agent list and Agent profile; web/native route and deep-link parity.

Acceptance: source-faithful UI retained; canonical FastAPI APIs supply real session/profile/relationship data; no fabricated counts/statuses; ownership/privacy enforced server-side; Web PWA and React Native + Expo navigation/deep links remain consistent; loading/empty/error/unauthenticated states are explicit; tests and evidence cover the changed journeys.

**Phase gate:** UI-COPY-03 is closed only for the UI copy/responsive-layout scope above. WP-02 is active. Do not jump to WP-03 until WP-02 meets its own acceptance criteria. Marketplace/backend and Vercel deployment gaps are not blockers for beginning WP-02 and must be handled by their designated later work packages/release process.


**WP-02 execution update — 2026-10-27 (shared session + profile lifecycle audit):**
- Audited `ReferenceAppShell`, `ReferenceNavigation`, `Navbar`, `BottomNav`, `/profile`, `/account`, `/messages`, `/notifications`, `/agents`, and the public Agent Passport route. Existing reference layout and route components remain the source of truth; no redesign or new DB introduced.
- `ReferenceNavigation` now observes the canonical Supabase Auth session, refreshes inbox indicators from `GET /v1/notifications?limit=1` and `GET /v1/messages/conversations`, and only displays indicator data if the API actually returns the documented numeric fields. It clears session-scoped indicators on logout and does not synthesize unread counts.
- `/profile` now reloads canonical `/v1/me` and `/v1/agents` on Auth session changes, clears prior identity/owned Agent data on logout, rejects an identity response that does not match the active session user, and uses a request sequence guard to avoid stale responses overwriting a newer session. Loading, unauthenticated and unavailable-profile states are now ordered explicitly to avoid rendering a false logged-out state during initial session resolution.
- Commits: [shared navigation session/inbox binding](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/commit/a467a490e7b619f3217009701b3471320fea5ea2), [profile session lifecycle](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/commit/08c89d4064dc3ddc37172b57b126b4086a8c4094), [profile loading/identity state correction](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/commit/0a3b570b57261e10f3043c8b2e81bc0c024f5208).
- CI is running on the latest WP-02 commit; no claim of validation is made until TypeScript, build, smoke and route checks finish. Inbox unread-message count remains unavailable unless the canonical conversations response exposes `unread_count`; social counts, profile-owned Feed/Reels data and Agent profile details must not be invented. Continue by verifying canonical response schemas, route/deep-link parity and remaining WP-02 acceptance tests.
- WP-02 remains **IN_PROGRESS**; do not start WP-03 before its acceptance gate passes.

**ENG-P04 — Social Graph implementation continuation — 2026-10-27 (API idempotency + preference parity):**
- Canonical source remains the existing Supabase tables `public.social_edges` and `public.user_interests`, with their existing RLS, unique constraints, follow-status trigger and Realtime publication. No new database, duplicate relationship registry or schema migration was introduced.
- [b483daa](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/commit/b483daa282135d82b80c1cac8e8b1436231afced) adds actor/target/type-scoped read-before-write to `POST /v1/me/social-edges`; an existing edge returns a stable idempotent result, and a concurrent unique-conflict is reconciled by reading the winning edge. Outcome is derived from canonical database state: active follow, pending private-profile request, block or mute. [69e201f](https://github.com/urbanrealty36-ops/Allpha-AI-Aplikasi/commit/69e201f84eedfc2da082c4551efa4aae18c22e84) returns HTTP 200 for idempotent replays and HTTP 201 for newly created edges.

## Execution update (2026-10-27, latest Feed/Native CI and grant review)

### Actual CI evidence and correction
- GitHub Actions run `40537136102` (Allpha Smoke, commit `aa5d6bf1468d5d346a3f2e1dcb93a6e7e751fab1`) reported `api-tests=success`, `native-client-checks=success`, and `web-build=failure`. The failing Web smoke assertion at `apps/web/scripts/smoke-test.mjs:527` incorrectly searched for the literal substring `!authEventSeen`; the Native implementation correctly guards the pending initial session read with `if (!active || authEventSeen) return;` and sets `authEventSeen = true;` inside `onAuthStateChange`. This was a test-contract mismatch, not evidence that the runtime guard was absent.
- Corrected the smoke assertion in commit `beea42e0f8186600949cb4c2ee27999f60e39247` to assert the actual guard and auth-event assignment. Follow-up commits strengthen the `user_settings` migration and its contract test: `8ad17086a1aed03bb18c751e4c642650bb719b41` and `3bde8f15e8c2ac768d3445cf3382d6465b02d7d5`.
- A new CI batch was observed in progress after the assertion fix. The latest head must be rechecked before claiming the fix passed; prior failures remain historical evidence until a new run completes. The current CI logs show that the earlier Web typecheck itself passed; the build's nested `npm test` failed at the same stale-session assertion before `next build` could run.
- Vercel status checks continue to show `build-rate-limit`; no Vercel deployment was attempted.

### Forward migration review — live evidence (read-only)
- Latest read-only `has_table_privilege` results:
  - `anon` on `public.user_settings`: SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE all true; UPDATE on `user_id` and `updated_at` true.
  - `authenticated` on `public.user_settings`: SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE all true; UPDATE on `user_id` and `updated_at` true.
  - `anon` on `public.social_edges` and `public.user_interests`: table privileges all false after the existing anonymous-revoke migration.
  - `authenticated` on both Social Graph tables: TRUNCATE remains true. `social_edges` has table SELECT/INSERT/DELETE and column-level INSERT/SELECT/REFERENCES grants, plus UPDATE(status); `user_interests` has table SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE and broad column-level INSERT/UPDATE/SELECT/REFERENCES grants.
- RLS policies are still required but do not mitigate TRUNCATE. The existing `20261027180000_eng_p03_user_settings_least_privilege_grants.sql` was tightened so its column-level revoke includes `PUBLIC, anon, authenticated`, not only `anon, authenticated`. This is source-only; the live ledger still lacks version `20261027180000`, and no production DDL was executed.
- Required new Social Graph forward migration design (not yet created or applied): revoke table-level and column-level privileges from `PUBLIC, anon, authenticated` on `public.social_edges` and `public.user_interests`; restore only `SELECT, DELETE` table privileges and `INSERT(actor_user_id, target_user_id, edge_type, status)` plus `UPDATE(status)` column privileges for `social_edges`; restore `SELECT, DELETE` on `user_interests`, `INSERT(user_id, interest_key, preference)`, and `UPDATE(user_id, interest_key, preference)` for authenticated users. The latter update columns preserve the existing atomic PostgREST `resolution=merge-duplicates` upsert contract while keeping `created_at` and `updated_at` client-read-only. Existing self/participant RLS policies remain in place. This SQL design must be committed as a newly generated migration and covered by source contract tests before it can be reviewed for application.
- The environment currently has no local checkout or Supabase CLI available, and a GitHub clone attempt failed because `github.com` DNS could not be resolved from the execution container. Per Supabase workflow requirements, do not invent a migration filename or bypass `supabase migration new`; the Social Graph migration file is therefore still a blocking deliverable, not “completed”. Resume file creation only in a networked repo environment where the migration can be generated by the CLI.

## ENG-P04 CLI-generated Social Graph grant migration (2026-10-27)

- Added `.github/workflows/eng-p04-generate-social-graph-migration.yml`. It installs Supabase CLI `2.81.3` on a networked GitHub Actions runner and invokes `supabase migration new eng_p04_social_graph_least_privilege_grants` from `apps/api`; it then copies the reviewed draft SQL into the CLI-created file and runs static contract checks. This avoids inventing a timestamp locally and avoids the execution container's DNS restriction.
- The generated migration now exists at `apps/api/supabase/migrations/20261027163225_eng_p04_social_graph_least_privilege_grants.sql`. The CLI-generated file revokes table and column grants from `PUBLIC, anon, authenticated`, then restores only the grants required by current FastAPI/PostgREST operations. It does not change schema, rows, RLS policies, or default privileges.
- Added a source contract test in `apps/api/tests/test_eng_p04_privacy_migration_contract.py` that requires exactly one CLI-generated migration and asserts the exact table/column revocations, required grants, and absence of TRUNCATE grants, broad grants, default-privilege changes, policy drops, or table drops.
- First workflow run `40542069756` failed after locating the already-generated migration because `pytest` was not installed in the runner. The workflow was corrected to set up Python 3.12 and install `apps/api` dev dependencies before running the contract test. The next generator run `40542341378` and Allpha Smoke run `40542341522` were queued at the latest check; their conclusions are pending, so do not report them as green until completed.
- The generator is source-only and does not connect to or apply DDL to production. The production least-privilege grant migration was applied on 2026-10-28 and recorded in the Supabase migration ledger; current verification is limited to grants/catalog checks. ENG-P04 remains `in_progress` pending green CI, reconciliation of the repository migration source with the applied ledger entry, and accepted two-user JWT/API/Realtime isolation evidence.
